> **10-28 10:45 Atlas: HT27 root causes verified by a fresh build of `atlas/z-ht27-triage` (6 tests, all red for the stated reason). Three corrections for HT27F; use these, not the table's cause column:**
> - **R2** is not a sort bug. `positioning_of` (`engine lib.rs:8387-8403`) deliberately maps `Relative => Static` ("entering the positioned paint path wrecks pages"), so `z-index:10` is never copied and `get_paint_order` (`layout lib.rs:6761-6795`) puts the relative box in normal flow under the abspos bucket. The fix is in the paint path: treat `style.position != Static` as positioned in `get_paint_order` and carry z-index, then re-check the pages that comment feared (campaign + A/B are the gate).
> - **R3**'s real root: `<img>` LayoutBoxes never get `transfer_positioning` (`engine lib.rs:9540-9557` returns early), so `reanchor_absolute` skips them and `.c`'s `top:0;left:0` are ignored; and `reanchor_absolute` only re-runs `calculate_block_height`, never `layout_image_in`. Both are needed: transfer positioning onto image boxes (hit-test and paint buckets change for every positioned img, so campaign + A/B) and a replaced-element branch in `reanchor_absolute` that re-runs `layout_image_in(.., Some(cb.content.height))`.
> - **N1**: the clamps are `flex.rs` ~1551/1567 `.max(0.0)`, and step 13 reads the abspos child's pre-pass size because `reanchor_absolute_children` (`layout lib.rs:2077`) runs after it. Allow negative free space AND run step 13's abspos alignment after (or from) the re-anchor.
> - R1 and N2 are as stated. N2 is Atlas's today.

> **2026-10-26 10:25 (Atlas): #788 gate accepted, good receipt; I'm landing it after one more independent review. Your #777 work is accepted too; it waits on Prometheus's re-stamp. DECISION: yes, the yahoo blank page is a D0 item, and the lead is the bug you found. If a page removes `<html>` and appends a new one, the engine throws HierarchyRequestError. Per the DOM spec that's allowed (a Document may have one element child, and after the removal it has none). If that append throws, the document has no root, so `document.head` is null, which is exactly yahoo's two TypeErrors at `document.head.appendChild`. Next, in order: (1) red-before tests for removing and re-appending `documentElement`, with `document.head`/`body` resolving to the new tree afterwards, plus `replaceChild` on the root and a doctype-order check; (2) the fix in the pre-insert validity checks; (3) yahoo `diagnose` on develop + #777 + the fix, compared with develop alone; (4) campaign + the 20-site A/B. > **Atlas 12:15 ET Tue. #735 CHANGE (Pete's standing rule, root cause over revert; ZeuzGb #70, Trace Null #19): do NOT revert 6bd612c9's shared h2; fix it.**
> (a) Honor the peer's SETTINGS_MAX_CONCURRENT_STREAMS: queue streams beyond the limit, and cap `initial_max_send_streams` until SETTINGS is read. Walmart's burst of about 113 scripts gets REFUSED_STREAM or GOAWAY at 100 today.
> (b) Retry REFUSED_STREAM, and GOAWAY streams above last_stream_id, ONCE on a fresh connection, for idempotent requests only. RFC 9113 §8.7 says the server never processed them. This is ordinary protocol behaviour that Chrome also has. It is NOT challenge handling, and it is the only retry allowed.
> (c) Find the head-of-line hang: one stuck request holding the host's other requests until the 30 s timeout. Look for a lock or serialisation around the shared SendRequest or connection driver.
> Tests: a local h2 server fixture with MAX_CONCURRENT_STREAMS=2, a refused stream, a GOAWAY, and one stalled stream that must not delay the others. Then Handtest runs walmart and microsoft live with h2=debug on the branch, on top of #743 (now on develop). Revert only if (a)-(c) cannot be made correct in about two sessions.

> **Atlas 23:50 ET Fri. NEXT WEEK'S RULES, from Codex's process review (Pete relayed it; Atlas agrees with five of its six points). These apply from Monday and sit on top of the Monday order below.**
> 1. **One behavior, one contract, one owner.** The "script scheduling" behavior covers this week's 0-scripts budget fix, the #716 Reddit rework and Chrono24's first-load fetch; they are one behavior, not three jobs. The behavior's contract goes in `docs/design/SCRIPT_SCHEDULING_CONTRACT.md` before more code lands. For each script kind (parser, async, defer, module, inserted, inserted with src set later), it says what starts it, what states it can enter, what completes it, what cancels it (budget, navigation, stop), and what the page observes in each case (load/error events, execution order, `document.readyState`). The Z lane owns the integrated result: it writes the contract Monday morning and reviews the Grok weekend branches against it. The Grok seats implement and measure the pieces. #707's mistake was a completion boundary that was too small; this rule is the fix.
> 2. **At most two major behavioral changes in flight:** script scheduling, and M4 (PR1, the protocol, is a draft only). Fixtures, measurements and isolated fixes are not limited. Nothing else that touches the page lifecycle starts until one of the two lands.
> 3. **Regression rule:** a regression on develop is fixed, disabled behind a flag, or reverted within one working day of being localised. It is never the base for new features. Applied now: if the budget split is not on develop by end of Monday, the inserted-script wait from #707/#712 goes behind a default-off flag until it is.
> 4. **Task acceptance suite (the main change).** A set of everyday tasks, each run 5 times on the same build, scored as N/5, with every failure recorded: read an article, scroll, follow a link and go back; search and filter listings, open several results; type and edit in a form with keyboard and mouse; switch tabs and keep page state; stop a slow page and keep browsing; resize, zoom, copy text, and recover from a failed navigation. Use about 8 sites from the board, no ebay.com. Handtest drives it in the real window only when Pete says the Mac is free; Iris diffs the results. The lane drafts the suite this week (`trench/z/ACCEPTANCE_TASKS.md`). **Atlas recommends it becomes Project A's pass criterion alongside section 10A. That changes the approved plan, so it is Pete's call.** The first-screen board stays as a secondary signal.
> 5. **Standards tests.** Add a vendored subset of Web Platform Tests for script execution (`html/semantics/scripting-1/the-script-element`) and DOM events, run headless in CI and recorded as a pass count. If the Z pilot's WPT sweep ledger exists, extend it rather than starting over. Each PR in the two areas above reports the WPT delta beside its own fixtures and the task-suite result. We collect three kinds of evidence, each answering a different question: is the behavior correct (WPT), does this bug stay fixed (fixtures), does the browser work (tasks).
> 6. **Clean baseline.** The flaky `control::tests::a_short_turn_opened_while_the_watchdog_sleeps_a_tick_is_stopped_on_time` (it turned develop red on Friday) gets fixed Monday. REALITY vs row: chrome.html has ONE backdrop-filter rule (`body.peek-mode .toolbar`), and it blurs chrome.html only — the chrome view cannot blur the web page beneath it (same in Chromium/WebKit); blurring the page under the toolbar would mean compositing the content view into the chrome's backdrop → not in scope (A3d candidate, only if Pete wants the effect). (a) Page scripts off the window thread, per the thread-ownership design merged as #652 (correct the doc per its HOLD review first); the window keeps drawing, scrolling and taking clicks while a script runs. The engine does not run keyframe animations at all (nothing outside `rustkit-animation` uses `KeyframesRule`/`Animation`; `@keyframes` is parsed and dropped), and `group_opacity` (layout lib.rs:9084) deliberately paints ANY box naming an animation at opacity 1 so the `opacity:0; animation: fadeIn forwards` idiom is not invisible for good. So reddit's `.75` is ignored by that guard, not by a t=0 keyframe. Choose: (a) keep the guard but only when the named `@keyframes` sets `opacity` (needs `@keyframes` carried to layout; reddit's 100% frame sets opacity, so reddit stays at 1); (b) a static "settled" frame: finite + forwards/both → last keyframe's opacity, otherwise the box's own opacity unless the 0% frame sets one (reddit → .75, fade-in → 1); (c) run CSS animations for real (multi-session). Lane pick: (b), one PR, red test = reddit disc shape. |

## Category board (B0), first list, Atlas's picks pending Pete's edits
Six categories of five. A site that is refused at the network layer moves to the blocked row and is reported there, never quietly dropped.
- search/portals: google, bing, duckduckgo, yahoo, msn
- reference/docs: wikipedia, github, developer.mozilla.org, stackoverflow, docs.python.org
- news/editorial: cnn, bbc, theguardian, npr, simonwillison
- shopping/listings: walmart, etsy, cargurus, autotrader, bringatrailer
- social/forums: reddit, x, youtube, news.ycombinator, facebook
- apps/product: shopify, squarespace, lyft, microsoft, apple
- blocked row (network refusal with the board's honest handshake): ebay, amazon, chrono24, cars, nytimes, chatgpt. ebay stays on Pete's hand-test list, where the app loads it.

## Land law and reviewers
- Land when: R1 CLEAR and R2 PASS at the same head, checks green, merge state CLEAN. Atlas merges one PR per command with `--match-head-commit`. Never force-push. Master only on Pete's word.
- R2 stamps come from Cursor as comments OR reviews; check both.
- **`no-check` label (Pete, 2026-10-12):** a PR carrying the GitHub label `no-check` is a standing or parked PR (first: #570, the Trace board ledger). Nobody reviews it, stamps it, runs a receipt on it or merges it, and it is left out of status lists and review requests. Removing the label puts it back in the queue. Atlas applies and removes the label; a seat that wants one parked asks.
- Stand-in first reviewer (Pete, 2026-10-09): a fresh Claude cloud session with no author context may give the first review when Prometheus has not answered within two hours; its body starts `R1 CLEAR @ <sha8>` or `R1 HOLD @ <sha8>`. This week it is used first for #661-#663.
- hiwave-macos is public: no host paths, ports, security details or live-site screenshots in PRs or issues. Frames go to hiwave-renders-private.
## Closure gates (B0, C0, C1, C2, D0-SVG, D1-L0), on top of the land law
1. Reduced failing case committed before the fix, green after.
2. receipt.py output in the PR body.
3. 26-case campaign at the candidate SHA (identical or every mover explained); all-site A/B for paint/fetch/script changes.
4. C1/C2: XHR_FETCH_DESIGN §7 deny matrix passes; no connection before the vet.
5. Independent re-run of the acceptance claim on the merged binary by a non-author seat that can run it: PROMETHEUS on the Mac for macOS closures (exact merged SHA, release build through the lease, own worktree); Pollux re-runs the Windows side on the next refresh; Argos (or Prometheus while Argos is silent) for Linux. Posted on the exchange.

## Receipt step for seat and cloud PRs
Athena and Talos cannot run macOS gates. For every seat PR to hiwave-macos that reaches R1 CLEAR, the Z lane runs a RECEIPT STEP on the Mac before its own package work: build the PR head with --profile parity, run the 26-case campaign and (for paint/fetch/script changes) the all-site A/B against develop, and post the result on the PR. The seat posts its own OS's parity and script-log A/B, labelled with the OS. Nobody claims a result they did not run.
- receipt.py: hiwave-macos scripts/receipt.py (plain Python, all OSes); until it lands, the same fields by hand.
- Cloud pilot PRs (branches cloud/*, amended 2026-10-03): same rule. To keep it to ONE lane run: when several cloud PRs are R1 CLEAR and confined to rustkit-bindings/rustkit-js with green pr-swarm (the CI's macOS 26-case run), merge their heads into one throwaway local branch, run the all-site A/B of that against develop once, and post the same result on each PR naming the SHAs covered. If the combined run regresses a site, bisect by PR. A cloud PR does not land without this.

## Rules
- Hand-test findings outrank headless scores. A fix for a hand-test item is not landed until the real-window driver shows it in the window.
- Progress is reported as user tasks that work, board points and H-items closed, never as commit counts.
- Thresholds, baselines and the scorer do not move without Pete. New measurements publish beside the old board.
- Honest networking as amended 2026-10-06: the app may match Chrome's handshake shape; no User-Agent change, no challenge answering; the board keeps the old handshake by default; one load per site per run.
- Stop rule: two consecutive sessions on one package with no landed receipt -> state=blocked + one-line decision packet in digest-z.md; the lane takes the next package.
- Builds: every cargo command via rs-cargo.py (lease + sccache). --profile parity for pixels, --release for timing.
- No pings 17:00-19:30 ET. One noon digest per day. LLM seats dormant Fri 00:00 to Mon 06:00 ET.
- Thursday night is the baseline; fixes continue to Friday midnight; Pete hand-tests on the weekend and his notes are Monday's queue.
